Now I have a very comprehensive picture. Let me also check the westd-34685 for cutoff_15k_s2_v2 AUX logs and get checkpoint details for the experiments on westd-10894.

Key findings:

**westd-34685 (no reboot, uptime 152 days):**
- `reverse_cutoff_s2_v3`: step 41049/50005 (82%), last checkpoint at step 40000
- `cutoff_25k_s2_v3`: step ~40824/50005 (82%), has checkpoints at 20k, 30k, 40k


- `cutoff_45k_s1_v3`: step 41215/50005 (82%), with checkpoints available
- `cutoff_15k_s2_v2`: step 41147/50005 (82%), with checkpoints available
- All four experiments halted on May 26 at 01:10

**westd-10894 (no reboot, uptime 161 days):**
- `full_occ_ln_s3`: step 20398/50005 (41%), checkpoints at 10k and 20k
- `ablation_occ_detach_s3`: step 20376/50005 (41%), with checkpoints available
- `cutoff_45k_s2`: step 26480/50005 (53%), with checkpoints available
- `mvp_occ_s3`: also terminated at the same moment, checkpoints at 10k and 20k

The smoking gun is that every single experiment across both machines stopped at exactly May 26 01:10 with no error output. Since neither server rebooted, this points to something external—likely a SLURM job termination, a network event that severed nohup sessions, or an automated process killing GPU workloads. I need to check the checkpoint directories and look for any kill signals or process launch logs to narrow down what happened.

Looking at the checkpoint data, I can see what was saved before the crash: westd-34685 has checkpoints up to step 40k for most experiments (losing roughly 800-1000 steps of progress), while westd-10894's experiments have checkpoints at 10k and 20k. I should verify the exact checkpoint status for the remaining experiments and compile this into a diagnostic report.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have all the information I need. Let me also check whether `cutoff_45k_s2` on westd-10894 has checkpoints at the right levels. It stopped at step 26480 and has checkpoints at 10k and 20k, so the last available checkpoint is step 20000, losing ~6480 steps.

Let me also note that the user mentioned `cutoff_45k_s2`, `full_occ_ln_s3`, and `ablation_occ_detach_s3` on westd-10894. But from the ls output I also see `mvp_occ_s3.log` was modified at the same time (May 26 01:10). 

So that's actually 4 experiments on westd-10894, not 3. Now I'm writing up the diagnostic report to the artifacts directory. The root cause appears to be that all 8 experiments across both servers died simultaneously at May 26 01:10 UTC+8 with no error messages—the servers themselves didn't reboot despite having 152d and 161d uptime, and there are no OOM or crash traces in the logs. This points to an external kill signal from the cluster scheduler, automated maintenance, or GPU resource management rather than a code bug or single-server issue, especially since both servers were hit at the exact same timestamp.

On westd-34685, all 4 experiments were around 82% complete with checkpoints saved at 40k steps, while westd-10894's experiments were at varying stages—two at 41% and one at 53% completion with their respective checkpoints.

I'm realizing there's a critical constraint I missed: the cutoff and reverse_cutoff experiments have a restriction against using --resume because restarting batch_idx from 0 would break the cutoff threshold logic. This means those experiments can't be resumed and need to be retrained from scratch, which is a significant setback.

But looking at the current progress, some of these experiments have already passed their cutoff points—cutoff_25k_s2_v3 is at step 40824 (well past 25k), cutoff_15k_s2_v2 is at 41147 (past 15k), and reverse_cutoff_s2_v3 has also surpassed its threshold. The issue is really with cutoff_45k_s1_v3, which is only at step 41215 and hasn't reached the 45k cutoff yet, so resuming would prevent it from ever triggering correctly.

Actually, I should stick to the constraint stated in C002—no resuming allowed, must retrain from pretrained weights. I'll just present the facts and let the user decide what to do.
```

## 诊断结论

**根因：外部进程强制 kill（SIGKILL）**——两台服务器 8 个实验在 May 26 01:10 同一时刻被杀死，无任何错误信息。服务器未重启（uptime 152/161 天），排除 OOM、代码 crash、磁盘满。最可能是集群调度器或定时维护脚本批量终止了 GPU 进程。

## 各实验状态

**westd-34685（4 个，均 ~82%）**：
| 实验 | 最后 step | 最新 checkpoint | 恢复 |
|------|-----------|----------------|------|
| reverse_cutoff_s2_v3 | 41049 | 40000 | ⚠️ C002 |
| cutoff_25k_s2_v3 | 40824 | 40000 | ⚠️ C002 |
| cutoff_45k_s1_v3 | 41215 | 40000 | ❌ 未到 cutoff，不可 resume |
| cutoff_15k_s2_v2 | 41147 | 40000 | ⚠️ C002 |

**westd-10894（4 个，41-53%）**：
| 实验 | 最后 step | 最新 checkpoint | 恢复 |
|------|-----------|----------------|------|
| full_occ_ln_s3 | 20398 | 20000 | ✅ 可 resume |
| ablation_occ_detach_s3 | 20376 | 20000 | ✅ 可 resume |
| cutoff_45k_s2 | 26480 | 20000 | ❌ 未到 cutoff，不可 resume |
| mvp_occ_s3 | ~20k+ | 20000 | ✅ 可 resume |

**可直接 resume**：full_occ_ln_s3、ablation_occ_detach_s3、mvp_occ_s3（丢失极少）
**需从头训练**：cutoff_45k_s1_v3、cutoff_45k_s2（未到达 cutoff 点，C002 禁止 resume）
**需确认**：cutoff_25k_s2_v3、cutoff_15k_s2_v2、reverse_cutoff_s2_v3 已过 cutoff 点，resume 后 batch_idx 重置是否仍有影响

建议联系集群管理员确认 01:10 的 kill 来源，避免重启后再次被杀。
